| **[D-1186] [process-gap] `pr-manager-completion-guard` SubagentStop hook defect — no terminal state for a scoped NON-merge pr-manager dispatch** | **OPEN 2026-09-09 — anchored next engine-discipline self-improvement cycle or a dedicated hook-hardening story (E-12, no story ID allocated yet)** | Discovered during PR #824's review-convergence cascade this session: the `pr-manager-completion-guard` SubagentStop hook has no terminal state it recognizes for a scoped NON-merge pr-manager dispatch (e.g. a pr-manager instance dispatched only to run one review cycle, not to complete the full 9-step PR lifecycle) and instead increments its internal "next step" counter uncapped, past the real 9-step lifecycle boundary — this produced an infinite stop-loop this session, requiring the orchestrator to reap the stuck subagent manually. Fix: the hook must recognize an explicit halted/blocked terminal state for scoped dispatches and cap step-increment at the actual lifecycle boundary (9 steps). Not fixed this burst — state-manager does not own hook source (`crates/`); routed to the E-12 Engine Governance follow-up story / next hook-hardening pass. |

Fix: harden `exec_subprocess`'s exit-code capture/propagation path to be correct under contention (e.g. avoid a race on the child's exit-status read). Not fixed this burst — out of state-manager's scope (crate source); routed to the E-12 Engine Governance follow-up story / next hook-hardening pass, alongside the D-1186 `pr-manager-completion-guard` item above. |
